# Storage: rejected and dead

> Where bytes can live: object storage, drives, registries, academic repositories, hosts used as storage.

1,720 records: 371 usable, 33 trial, 103 paid only, 1,035 rejected, 108 dead; 70 self-hosted of any group, listed last. This page lists the rejected and dead records, each with the evidence that rejected it; the usable, trial and paid-only ones are on https://fshy.ajam.dev/t/storage.md. Each line: provider (its page in Markdown), record id (its JSON is https://fshy.ajam.dev/api/v1/record/<id>), verdict, free tier, identity, card, phone, evidence, stale date, parsed price, summary. In full: https://fshy.ajam.dev/t/storage/llms-full.txt.

Summaries are cut to 66 characters to keep this page within 300 KB, about 81K tokens; each provider page has them whole.
